│                                                                              │
│  1 In _schedule_swapped (line 737-742): Added a check to only call           │
│    budget.add_num_seqs when enable_chunking=True. This is consistent with    │
│    the behavior in _schedule_running (line 632-634) where                    │
│    budget.add_num_seqs is only called when enable_chunking=True. The comment │
│    explains that get_max_num_running_seqs is expensive, and for the default  │
│    scheduling case where enable_chunking=False, num_seqs are updated before  │
│    running this method, so we don't have to update it again here.            │
│  2 In _schedule_chunked_prefill (line 1022-1024): Added enable_chunking=True │
│    to the _schedule_swapped call. This ensures that when swapping in a       │
│    prefill request, the num_new_tokens will be chunked according to the      │
│    budget, which is consistent with how _schedule_running and                │
│    _schedule_prefills are called with enable_chunking=True.                  │
│                                                                              │
│ These changes improve the chunked prefill performance by:                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Avoiding unnecessary calls to get_max_num_running_seqs when               │
│    enable_chunking=False                                                     │
│  • Ensuring that swapped prefill requests are properly chunked according to  │
│    the budget when enable_chunking=True                                      │
